{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_017ri1PYzgTMXW2oR3MHnVcW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01HbRqQeLjRkU8F62US1vsFs","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/acopf_solver.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver\nImplements the mathematical model from math-model.md using CasADi and IPOPT\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nimport sys\n\ndef load_network_data(filename='network.json'):\n    \"\"\"Load network data from JSON file\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef parse_network_data(data):\n    \"\"\"Parse network data into structured format\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    bus = np.array(data['bus'])\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    bus_i = bus[:, 0].astype(int)  # Bus number\n    bus_type = bus[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=Slack\n    pd = bus[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Load MW -> pu\n    qd = bus[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Load MVAr -> pu\n    gs = bus[:, 4]  # Shunt conductance pu\n    bs = bus[:, 5]  # Shunt susceptance pu\n    vm_init = bus[:, 7]  # Initial voltage magnitude\n    va_init = bus[:, 8] * np.pi / 180  # Initial angle rad\n    vmax = bus[:, 11]  # Max voltage pu\n    vmin = bus[:, 12]  # Min voltage pu\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    gen = np.array(data['gen'])\n    n_gen = len(gen)\n    gen_bus = gen[:, 0].astype(int)  # Generator bus\n    pg_init = gen[:, 1] / baseMVA  # Initial P output pu\n    qg_init = gen[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Initial Q output pu\n    qmax = gen[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Max Q pu\n    qmin = gen[:, 4] / baseMVA  # Min Q pu\n    vg = gen[:, 5]  # Voltage setpoint pu\n    gen_status = gen[:, 7].astype(int)  # Status\n    pmax = gen[:, 8] / baseMVA  # Max P pu\n    pmin = gen[:, 9] / baseMVA  # Min P pu\n\n    # Parse generator cost\n    gencost = np.array(data['gencost'])\n    # Model 2 = polynomial: cost = c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    cost_model = gencost[:, 0].astype(int)\n    ncost = gencost[:, 3].astype(int)\n\n    # Extract quadratic cost coefficients (converting from $/h to pu)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if cost_model[i] == 2:  # Polynomial\n            if ncost[i] == 3:  # Quadratic\n                c2[i] = gencost[i, 4] * baseMVA**2  # $/MW^2/h -> $/pu^2/h\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] * baseMVA      # $/MW/h -> $/pu/h\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6]                # $/h\n            elif ncost[i] == 2:  # Linear\n                c2[i] = 0\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 4] * baseMVA\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 5]\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branch = np.array(data['branch'])\n    n_branch = len(branch)\n    f_bus = branch[:, 0].astype(int)  # From bus\n    t_bus = branch[:, 1].astype(int)  # To bus\n    br_r = branch[:, 2]  # Resistance pu\n    br_x = branch[:, 3]  # Reactance pu\n    br_b = branch[:, 4]  # Charging susceptance pu\n    rate_a = branch[:, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating -> pu\n    tap = branch[:, 8]  # Tap ratio\n    shift = branch[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # Phase shift deg -> rad\n    br_status = branch[:, 10].astype(int)  # Status\n    angmin = branch[:, 11] * np.pi / 180  # Min angle diff rad\n    angmax = branch[:, 12] * np.pi / 180  # Max angle diff rad\n\n    # Set default tap ratio to 1 if 0\n    tap[tap == 0] = 1.0\n\n    # Find reference bus (slack bus, type 3)\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_bus) == 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"No reference bus found\")\n    ref_bus = ref_bus[0]\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'bus_i': bus_i,\n        'bus_type': bus_type,\n        'pd': pd,\n        'qd': qd,\n        'gs': gs,\n        'bs': bs,\n        'vm_init': vm_init,\n        'va_init': va_init,\n        'vmax': vmax,\n        'vmin': vmin,\n        'gen_bus': gen_bus,\n        'pg_init': pg_init,\n        'qg_init': qg_init,\n        'qmax': qmax,\n        'qmin': qmin,\n        'vg': vg,\n        'gen_status': gen_status,\n        'pmax': pmax,\n        'pmin': pmin,\n        'c2': c2,\n        'c1': c1,\n        'c0': c0,\n        'f_bus': f_bus,\n        't_bus': t_bus,\n        'br_r': br_r,\n        'br_x': br_x,\n        'br_b': br_b,\n        'rate_a': rate_a,\n        'tap': tap,\n        'shift': shift,\n        'br_status': br_status,\n        'angmin': angmin,\n        'angmax': angmax,\n        'ref_bus': ref_bus,\n    }\n\ndef build_acopf(net):\n    \"\"\"Build AC OPF optimization problem using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    # Create optimization variables\n    # Pg, Qg for each generator (real power, reactive power)\n    pg = ca.MX.sym('pg', net['n_gen'])\n    qg = ca.MX.sym('qg', net['n_gen'])\n\n    # Vm, Va for each bus (voltage magnitude, angle)\n    vm = ca.MX.sym('vm', net['n_bus'])\n    va = ca.MX.sym('va', net['n_bus'])\n\n    # Collect all variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(pg, qg, vm, va)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    # sum_k c2_k * pg_k^2 + c1_k * pg_k + c0_k\n    obj = 0\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            obj += net['c2'][k] * pg[k]**2 + net['c1'][k] * pg[k] + net['c0'][k]\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Reference bus angle constraint: va[ref] = 0\n    g.append(va[net['ref_bus']])\n    lbg.append(0)\n    ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Create bus-to-generator mapping\n    bus_gens = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        bus_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['gen_bus'][k])[0][0]\n        bus_gens[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Create bus-to-branch mapping (branches connected to each bus)\n    bus_branches_from = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n    bus_branches_to = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            bus_branches_from[f_idx].append(b)\n            bus_branches_to[t_idx].append(b)\n\n    # Power balance equations at each bus\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        # Sum of generation at bus i\n        p_gen = 0\n        q_gen = 0\n        for k in bus_gens[i]:\n            if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n                p_gen += pg[k]\n                q_gen += qg[k]\n\n        # Load at bus i\n        p_load = net['pd'][i]\n        q_load = net['qd'][i]\n\n        # Shunt at bus i: S_shunt = (G - jB) * |V|^2\n        # So P_shunt = G * |V|^2, Q_shunt = -B * |V|^2\n        p_shunt = net['gs'][i] * vm[i]**2\n        q_shunt = -net['bs'][i] * vm[i]**2\n\n        # Sum of branch flows from bus i\n        p_flow = 0\n        q_flow = 0\n\n        # Branches where i is the \"from\" bus\n        for b in bus_branches_from[i]:\n            f_idx = i\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            # Branch admittance: y = 1/(r + jx) = (r - jx)/(r^2 + x^2)\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n            # Shunt admittance at from side\n            g_shunt_f = 0.0\n            b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            # Complex transformation ratio: T = tap * exp(j*shift)\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n            tap_mag_sq = tap_mag**2\n\n            # S_ij = (Y_ij + Y^c_ij)^* * |V_i|^2 / |T_ij|^2 - Y_ij^* * V_i * V_j^* / T_ij\n            # For real admittances (typical case):\n            # P_ij = (g+g_sh) * vm_i^2 / tap^2 - vm_i*vm_j/tap * (g*cos(va_i-va_j-shift) + b*sin(va_i-va_j-shift))\n            # Q_ij = -(b+b_sh) * vm_i^2 / tap^2 - vm_i*vm_j/tap * (g*sin(va_i-va_j-shift) - b*cos(va_i-va_j-shift))\n\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ij = ((g_series + g_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) + b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ij = (-(b_series + b_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) - b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            p_flow += p_ij\n            q_flow += q_ij\n\n        # Branches where i is the \"to\" bus\n        for b in bus_branches_to[i]:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = i\n\n            # Branch admittance\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n            # Shunt admittance at to side\n            g_shunt_t = 0.0\n            b_shunt_t = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n\n            # S_ji = (Y_ij + Y^c_ji)^* * |V_j|^2 - Y_ij^* * V_j * V_i^* / T_ij^*\n            # P_ji = (g+g_sh) * vm_j^2 - vm_i*vm_j/tap * (g*cos(va_i-va_j-shift) - b*sin(va_i-va_j-shift))\n            # Q_ji = -(b+b_sh) * vm_j^2 - vm_i*vm_j/tap * (g*sin(va_i-va_j-shift) + b*cos(va_i-va_j-shift))\n\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ji = ((g_series + g_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) - b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) + b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            p_flow += p_ji\n            q_flow += q_ji\n\n        # Power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n        g.append(p_gen - p_load - p_shunt - p_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(q_gen - q_load - q_shunt - q_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n    x0 = []\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            lbx.append(net['pmin'][k])\n            ubx.append(net['pmax'][k])\n            x0.append(net['pg_init'][k])\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            lbx.append(net['qmin'][k])\n            ubx.append(net['qmax'][k])\n            x0.append(net['qg_init'][k])\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        lbx.append(net['vmin'][i])\n        ubx.append(net['vmax'][i])\n        x0.append(net['vm_init'][i])\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (use wide bounds, except ref bus)\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n        x0.append(net['va_init'][i])\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1 and net['rate_a'][b] > 0:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            # Compute branch flow magnitude\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            g_shunt_f = 0.0\n            b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n            tap_mag_sq = tap_mag**2\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ij = ((g_series + g_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) + b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ij = (-(b_series + b_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) - b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            s_ij_mag = ca.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n\n            h.append(s_ij_mag)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(net['rate_a'][b])\n\n            # Also add reverse direction\n            g_shunt_t = 0.0\n            b_shunt_t = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            p_ji = ((g_series + g_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) - b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) + b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            s_ji_mag = ca.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n            h.append(s_ji_mag)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(net['rate_a'][b])\n\n    # Angle difference limits\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx]\n\n            h.append(va_diff)\n            lbh.append(net['angmin'][b])\n            ubh.append(net['angmax'][b])\n\n    # Concatenate constraints\n    g_all = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h_all = ca.vertcat(*h) if h else ca.MX()\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    if g and h:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(g_all, h_all)\n        lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n        ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n    elif g:\n        constraints = g_all\n        lbg_all = lbg\n        ubg_all = ubg\n    elif h:\n        constraints = h_all\n        lbg_all = lbh\n        ubg_all = ubh\n    else:\n        constraints = ca.MX()\n        lbg_all = []\n        ubg_all = []\n\n    return {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': constraints,\n        'x0': np.array(x0),\n        'lbx': np.array(lbx),\n        'ubx': np.array(ubx),\n        'lbg': np.array(lbg_all),\n        'ubg': np.array(ubg_all),\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(net):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Building AC OPF problem...\")\n    nlp = build_acopf(net)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {nlp['x'].shape[0]} variables, {nlp['g'].shape[0]} constraints\")\n\n    # Create NLP solver\n    opts = 'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.linear_solver': 'mumps',\n        'print_time': 1,\n    }\n\n    nlp_dict = {\n        'x': nlp['x'],\n        'f': nlp['f'],\n        'g': nlp['g'],\n      lbg=nlp['lbg'],\n        ubg=nlp['ubg'],\n    )\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n\n    pg_opt = x_opt[0:net['n_gen']]\n    qg_opt = branch_flows = []\n\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n        t_idx pu\n    gs = bus[:, 4]  # Shunt conductance pu\n    bs = bus[:, 5]  # Shunt susceptance pu\n    vm_init = bus[:, 7]  # Initial voltage magnitude\n    va_init = bus[:, 8] * np.pi / 180  # Initial angle rad\n    vmax = bus[:, 11]  # Max voltage pu\n    vmin = bus[:, 12]  # Min voltage pu\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    gen = np.array(data['gen'])\n    n_gen = len(gen)\n    gen_bus = gen[:, 0].astype(int)  # Generator bus\n    pg_init = gen[:, 1] / baseMVA  # Initial P output pu\n    qg_init = gen[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Initial Q output pu\n    qmax = gen[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Max Q pu\n    qmin = gen[:, 4] / baseMVA  # Min Q pu\n    vg = gen[:, 5]  # Voltage setpoint pu\n    gen_status = gen[:, 7].astype(int)  # Status\n    pmax = gen[:, 8] / baseMVA  # Max P pu\n    pmin = gen[:, 9] / baseMVA  # Min P pu\n\n    # Parse generator cost\n    gencost = np.array(data['gencost'])\n    # Model 2 = polynomial: cost = c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\n    cost_model = gencost[:, 0].astype(int)\n    ncost = gencost[:, 3].astype(int)\n\n    # Extract quadratic cost coefficients (converting from $/h to pu)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if cost_model[i] == 2:  # Polynomial\n            if ncost[i] == 3:  # Quadratic\n                c2[i] = gencost[i, 4] * baseMVA**2  # $/MW^2/h -> $/pu^2/h\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] * baseMVA      # $/MW/h -> $/pu/h\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6]                # $/h\n            elif ncost[i] == 2:  # Linear\n                c2[i] = 0\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 4] * baseMVA\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 5]\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branch = np.array(data['branch'])\n    n_branch = len(branch)\n    f_bus = branch[:, 0].astype(int)  # From bus\n    t_bus = branch[:, 1].astype(int)  # To bus\n    br_r = branch[:, 2]  # Resistance pu\n    br_x = branch[:, 3]  # Reactance pu\n    br_b = branch[:, 4]  # Charging susceptance pu\n    rate_a = branch[:, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating -> pu\n    tap = branch[:, 8]  # Tap ratio\n    shift = branch[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # Phase shift deg -> rad\n    br_status = branch[:, 10].astype(int)  # Status\n    angmin = branch[:, 11] * np.pi / 180  # Min angle diff rad\n    angmax = branch[:, 12] * np.pi / 180  # Max angle diff rad\n\n    # Set default tap ratio to 1 if 0\n    tap[tap == 0] = 1.0\n\n    # Find reference bus (slack bus, type 3)\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_bus) == 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"No reference bus found\")\n    ref_bus = ref_bus[0]\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'bus_i': bus_i,\n        'bus_type': bus_type,\n        'pd': pd,\n        'qd': qd,\n        'gs': gs,\n        'bs': bs,\n        'vm_init': vm_init,\n        'va_init': va_init,\n        'vmax': vmax,\n        'vmin': vmin,\n        'gen_bus': gen_bus,\n        'pg_init': pg_init,\n        'qg_init': qg_init,\n        'qmax': qmax,\n        'qmin': qmin,\n        'vg': vg,\n        'gen_status': gen_status,\n        'pmax': pmax,\n        'pmin': pmin,\n        'c2': c2,\n        'c1': c1,\n        'c0': c0,\n        'f_bus': f_bus,\n        't_bus': t_bus,\n        'br_r': br_r,\n        'br_x': br_x,\n        'br_b': br_b,\n        'rate_a': rate_a,\n        'tap': tap,\n        'shift': shift,\n        'br_status': br_status,\n        'angmin': angmin,\n        'angmax': angmax,\n        'ref_bus': ref_bus,\n    }\n\ndef build_acopf(net):\n    \"\"\"Build AC OPF optimization problem using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    # Create optimization variables\n    # Pg, Qg for each generator (real power, reactive power)\n    pg = ca.MX.sym('pg', net['n_gen'])\n    qg = ca.MX.sym('qg', net['n_gen'])\n\n    # Vm, Va for each bus (voltage magnitude, angle)\n    vm = ca.MX.sym('vm', net['n_bus'])\n    va = ca.MX.sym('va', net['n_bus'])\n\n    # Collect all variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(pg, qg, vm, va)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    # sum_k c2_k * pg_k^2 + c1_k * pg_k + c0_k\n    obj = 0\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            obj += net['c2'][k] * pg[k]**2 + net['c1'][k] * pg[k] + net['c0'][k]\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Reference bus angle constraint: va[ref] = 0\n    g.append(va[net['ref_bus']])\n    lbg.append(0)\n    ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Create bus-to-generator mapping\n    bus_gens = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        bus_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['gen_bus'][k])[0][0]\n        bus_gens[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Create bus-to-branch mapping (branches connected to each bus)\n    bus_branches_from = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n    bus_branches_to = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            bus_branches_from[f_idx].append(b)\n            bus_branches_to[t_idx].append(b)\n\n    # Power balance equations at each bus\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        # Sum of generation at bus i\n        p_gen = 0\n        q_gen = 0\n        for k in bus_gens[i]:\n            if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n                p_gen += pg[k]\n                q_gen += qg[k]\n\n        # Load at bus i\n        p_load = net['pd'][i]\n        q_load = net['qd'][i]\n\n        # Shunt at bus i: S_shunt = (G - jB) * |V|^2\n        # So P_shunt = G * |V|^2, Q_shunt = -B * |V|^2\n        p_shunt = net['gs'][i] * vm[i]**2\n        q_shunt = -net['bs'][i] * vm[i]**2\n\n        # Sum of branch flows from bus i\n        p_flow = 0\n        q_flow = 0\n\n        # Branches where i is the \"from\" bus\n        for b in bus_branches_from[i]:\n            f_idx = i\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            # Branch admittance: y = 1/(r + jx) = (r - jx)/(r^2 + x^2)\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n            # Shunt admittance at from side\n            g_shunt_f = 0.0\n            b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            # Complex transformation ratio: T = tap * exp(j*shift)\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n            tap_mag_sq = tap_mag**2\n\n            # S_ij = (Y_ij + Y^c_ij)^* * |V_i|^2 / |T_ij|^2 - Y_ij^* * V_i * V_j^* / T_ij\n            # For real admittances (typical case):\n            # P_ij = (g+g_sh) * vm_i^2 / tap^2 - vm_i*vm_j/tap * (g*cos(va_i-va_j-shift) + b*sin(va_i-va_j-shift))\n            # Q_ij = -(b+b_sh) * vm_i^2 / tap^2 - vm_i*vm_j/tap * (g*sin(va_i-va_j-shift) - b*cos(va_i-va_j-shift))\n\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ij = ((g_series + g_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) + b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ij = (-(b_series + b_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) - b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            p_flow += p_ij\n            q_flow += q_ij\n\n        # Branches where i is the \"to\" bus\n        for b in bus_branches_to[i]:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = i\n\n            # Branch admittance\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n            # Shunt admittance at to side\n            g_shunt_t = 0.0\n            b_shunt_t = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n\n            # S_ji = (Y_ij + Y^c_ji)^* * |V_j|^2 - Y_ij^* * V_j * V_i^* / T_ij^*\n            # P_ji = (g+g_sh) * vm_j^2 - vm_i*vm_j/tap * (g*cos(va_i-va_j-shift) - b*sin(va_i-va_j-shift))\n            # Q_ji = -(b+b_sh) * vm_j^2 - vm_i*vm_j/tap * (g*sin(va_i-va_j-shift) + b*cos(va_i-va_j-shift))\n\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ji = ((g_series + g_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) - b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) + b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            p_flow += p_ji\n            q_flow += q_ji\n\n        # Power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n        g.append(p_gen - p_load - p_shunt - p_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(q_gen - q_load - q_shunt - q_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n    x0 = []\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            lbx.append(net['pmin'][k])\n            ubx.append(net['pmax'][k])\n            x0.append(net['pg_init'][k])\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            lbx.append(net['qmin'][k])\n            ubx.append(net['qmax'][k])\n            x0.append(net['qg_init'][k])\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        lbx.append(net['vmin'][i])\n        ubx.append(net['vmax'][i])\n        x0.append(net['vm_init'][i])\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (use wide bounds, except ref bus)\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n        x0.append(net['va_init'][i])\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1 and net['rate_a'][b] > 0:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            # Compute branch flow magnitude\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            g_shunt_f = 0.0\n            b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n            tap_mag_sq = tap_mag**2\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ij = ((g_series + g_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) + b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ij = (-(b_series + b_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) - b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            s_ij_mag = ca.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n\n            h.append(s_ij_mag)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(net['rate_a'][b])\n\n            # Also add reverse direction\n            g_shunt_t = 0.0\n            b_shunt_t = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            p_ji = ((g_series + g_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) - b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) + b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            s_ji_mag = ca.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n            h.append(s_ji_mag)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(net['rate_a'][b])\n\n    # Angle difference limits\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx]\n\n            h.append(va_diff)\n            lbh.append(net['angmin'][b])\n            ubh.append(net['angmax'][b])\n\n    # Concatenate constraints\n    g_all = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h_all = ca.vertcat(*h) if h else ca.MX()\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    if g and h:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(g_all, h_all)\n        lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n        ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n    elif g:\n        constraints = g_all\n        lbg_all = lbg\n        ubg_all = ubg\n    elif h:\n        constraints = h_all\n        lbg_all = lbh\n        ubg_all = ubh\n    else:\n        constraints = ca.MX()\n        lbg_all = []\n        ubg_all = []\n\n    return {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': constraints,\n        'x0': np.array(x0),\n        'lbx': np.array(lbx),\n        'ubx': np.array(ubx),\n        'lbg': np.array(lbg_all),\n        'ubg': np.array(ubg_all),\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(net):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Building AC OPF problem...\")\n    nlp = build_acopf(net)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {nlp['x'].shape[0]} variables, {nlp['g'].shape[0]} constraints\")\n\n    # Create NLP solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 15,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.linear_solver': 'mumps',\n        'print_time': 1,\n    }\n\n    nlp_dict = {\n        'x': nlp['x'],\n        'f': nlp['f'],\n        'g': nlp['g'],\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp_dict, opts)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC OPF...\")\n    sol = solver(\n        x0=nlp['x0'],\n        lbx=nlp['lbx'],\n        ubx=nlp['ubx'],\n        lbg=nlp['lbg'],\n        ubg=nlp['ubg'],\n    )\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n\n    pg_opt = x_opt[0:net['n_gen']]\n    qg_opt = x_opt[net['n_gen']:2*net['n_gen']]\n    vm_opt = x_opt[2*net['n_gen']:2*net['n_gen']+net['n_bus']]\n    va_opt = x_opt[2*net['n_gen']+net['n_bus']:2*net['n_gen']+2*net['n_bus']]\n\n    # Get solver stats\n    stats = solver.stats()\n\n    return {\n        'pg': pg_opt,\n        'qg': qg_opt,\n        'vm': vm_opt,\n        'va': va_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'stats': stats,\n        'success': stats['success'],\n    }\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(net, sol):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from solution\"\"\"\n\n    vm = sol['vm']\n    va = sol['va']\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n        t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n        # Branch admittance\n        y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n        g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n        b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n        g_shunt_f = 0.0\n        b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n        g_shunt_t = 0.0\n        b_shunt_t = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n        tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n        tap_mag_sq = tap_mag**2\n        shift = net['shift'][b]\n\n        va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - shift\n\n        # From side flow\n        p_ij = ((g_series + g_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * np.cos(va_diff) + b_series * np.sin(va_diff)))\n\n        q_ij = (-(b_series + b_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * np.sin(va_diff) - b_series * np.cos(va_diff)))\n\n        s_ij = np.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n\n        # To side flow\n        p_ji = ((g_series + g_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * np.cos(va_diff) - b_series * np.sin(va_diff)))\n\n        q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * np.sin(va_diff) + b_series * np.cos(va_diff)))\n\n        s_ji = np.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n        # Loading percentage\n        if net['rate_a'][b] > 0:\n            loading_pct = max(s_ij, s_ji) / net['rate_a'][b] * 100\n        else:\n            loading_pct = 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'branch_idx': b,\n            'from_bus': int(net['f_bus'][b]),\n            'to_bus': int(net['t_bus'][b]),\n            'p_from': p_ij * net['baseMVA'],\n            'q_from': q_ij * net['baseMVA'],\n            's_from': s_ij * net['baseMVA'],\n            'p_to': p_ji * net['baseMVA'],\n            'q_to': q_ji * net['baseMVA'],\n            's_to': s_ji * net['baseMVA'],\n            'limit': net['rate_a'][b] * net['baseMVA'],\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef check_feasibility(net, sol):\n    \"\"\"Check solution feasibility\"\"\"\n\n    vm = sol['vm']\n    va = sol['va']\n    pg = sol['pg']\n    qg = sol['qg']\n\n    # Create bus-to-generator mapping\n    bus_gens = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        bus_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['gen_bus'][k])[0][0]\n        bus_gens[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Create bus-to-branch mapping\n    bus_branches_from = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n    bus_branches_to = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            bus_branches_from[f_idx].append(b)\n            bus_branches_to[t_idx].append(b)\n\n    # Check power balance\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        # Generation\n        p_gen = sum(pg[k] for k in bus_gens[i] if net['gen_status'][k] == 1)\n        q_gen = sum(qg[k] for k in bus_gens[i] if net['gen_status'][k] == 1)\n\n        # Load\n        p_load = net['pd'][i]\n        q_load = net['qd'][i]\n\n        # Shunt\n        p_shunt = net['gs'][i] * vm[i]**2\n        q_shunt = -net['bs'][i] * vm[i]**2\n\n        # Branch flows\n        p_flow = 0\n        q_flow = 0\n\n        for b in bus_branches_from[i]:\n            f_idx = i\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            g_shunt_f = 0.0\n            b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n            tap_mag_sq = tap_mag**2\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ij = ((g_series + g_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * np.cos(va_diff) + b_series * np.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ij = (-(b_series + b_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * np.sin(va_diff) - b_series * np.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            p_flow += p_ij\n            q_flow += q_ij\n\n        for b in bus_branches_to[i]:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = i\n\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            g_shunt_t = 0.0\n            b_shunt_t = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ji = ((g_series + g_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * np.cos(va_diff) - b_series * np.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * np.sin(va_diff) + b_series * np.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            p_flow += p_ji\n            q_flow += q_ji\n\n        p_mismatch = abs(p_gen - p_load - p_shunt - p_flow)\n        q_mismatch = abs(q_gen - q_load - q_shunt - q_flow)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    # Check voltage limits\n    max_v_violation = 0\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        if vm[i] < net['vmin'][i]:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, net['vmin'][i] - vm[i])\n        if vm[i] > net['vmax'][i]:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, vm[i] - net['vmax'][i])\n\n    # Check branch flow limits\n    branch_flows = compute_branch_flows(net, sol)\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n    for bf in branch_flows:\n        if bf['limit'] > 0:\n            overload = max(bf['s_from'] - bf['limit'], bf['s_to'] - bf['limit'], 0)\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    return {\n        'max_p_mismatch_MW': max_p_mismatch * net['baseMVA'],\n        'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': max_q_mismatch * net['baseMVA'],\n        'max_voltage_violation_pu': max_v_violation,\n        'max_branch_overload_MVA': max_branch_overload,\n    }\n\ndef generate_report(net, sol, branch_flows, feasibility):\n    \"\"\"Generate JSON report\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = net['baseMVA']\n\n    # Summary\n    total_pg = sum(sol['pg']) * baseMVA\n    total_qg = sum(sol['qg']) * baseMVA\n    total_pd = sum(net['pd']) * baseMVA\n    total_qd = sum(net['qd']) * baseMVA\n    total_loss = total_pg - total_pd\n\n    solver_status = \"optimal\" if sol['success'] else \"failed\"\n\n    summary = {\n        'total_cost_per_hour': sol['cost'],\n        'total_load_MW': total_pd,\n        'total_load_MVAr': total_qd,\n        'total_generation_MW': total_pg,\n        'total_generation_MVAr': total_qg,\n        'total_losses_MW': total_loss,\n        'solver_status': solver_status,\n    }\n\n    # Generators\n    generators = []\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        generators.append({\n            'id': k + 1,\n            'bus': int(net['gen_bus'][k]),\n            'pg_MW': sol['pg'][k] * baseMVA,\n            'qg_MVAr': sol['qg'][k] * baseMVA,\n            'pmin_MW': net['pmin'][k] * baseMVA,\n            'pmax_MW': net['pmax'][k] * baseMVA,\n            'qmin_MVAr': net['qmin'][k] * baseMVA,\n            'qmax_MVAr': net['qmax'][k] * baseMVA,\n        })\n\n    # Buses\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        buses.append({\n            'id': int(net['bus_i'][i]),\n            'vm_pu': sol['vm'][i],\n            'va_deg': sol['va'][i] * 180 / np.pi,\n            'vmin_pu': net['vmin'][i],\n            'vmax_pu': net['vmax'][i],\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches (top 10)\n    sorted_branches = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = []\n    for bf in sorted_branches[:10]:\n        most_loaded.append({\n            'from_bus': bf['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': bf['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct': bf['loading_pct'],\n            'flow_from_MVA': bf['s_from'],\n            'flow_to_MVA': bf['s_to'],\n            'limit_MVA': bf['limit'],\n        })\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    feasibility_check = {\n        'max_p_mismatch_MW': feasibility['max_p_mismatch_MW'],\n        'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': feasibility['max_q_mismatch_MVAr'],\n        'max_voltage_violation_pu': feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'],\n        'max_branch_overload_MVA': feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'],\n    }\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': summary,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': feasibility_check,\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function\"\"\"\n\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"Loading network {net['n_gen']} generators, {net['n_branch']} branches\")\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    sol = solve_acopf(net)\n\n    if not sol['success']:\n        print(\"\\nERROR: Solver failed to find optimal solution\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"\\nOptimal cost: ${sol['cost']:.2f}/hour\")\n\n    # Compute branch flows\n    print(\"\\nComputing branch flows...\")\n    branch_flows = compute_branch_flows(net, sol)\n\n    # Check feasibility\n    print(\"Checking feasibility...\")\n    feasibility = check_feasibility(net, sol)\n\n    print(f\"Max P mismatch: report...\")\n    report = generate_report(net, sol, branch_flows, feasibility)\n\n    # Save report\n    with open('report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n / 2\n\n            p_ji = ((g_series + g_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) - b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) + b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            s_ji_mag = ca.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n            h.append(s_ji_mag)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(net['rate_a'][b])\n\n    # Angle difference limits\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if va[t_idx]\n\n            h.append(va_diff)\n            lbh.append(net['angmin'][b])\n            ubh.append(net['angmax'][b])\n\n    # Concatenate constraints\n    g_all = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h_all = ca.vertcat(*h) if h else ca.MX()\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    if g and h:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(g_all, h_all)\n        lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n        ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n    elif g:\n        constraints = g_all\n        lbg_all = lbg\n        ubg_all = ubg\n    elif h:\n        constraints = h_all\n        lbg_all = lbh\n        ubg_all = ubh\n    else:\n        constraints = ca.MX()\n        lbg_all = []\n        ubg_all = []\n\n    return {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': constraints,\n        'x0': np.array(x0),\n        'lbx': np.array(lbx),\n        'ubx': np.array(ubx),\n        'lbg': np.array(lbg_all),\n        'ubg': np.array(ubg_all),\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(net):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Building AC OPF problem...\")\n    nlp = build_acopf(net)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {nlp['x'].shape[0]} variables, {nlp['g'].shape[0]} constraints\")\n\n    # Create NLP solver\n    opts =      lbg=nlp['lbg'],\n        ubg=nlp['ubg'],\n    )\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n\n    pg_opt = x_opt[0:net['n_gen']]\n    qg_opt = x_opt[net['n_gen']:2*net['n_gen']]\n    vm_opt = x_opt[2*net['n_gen']:2*net['n_gen']+net['n_bus']]\n    va_opt = x_opt[2*net['n_gen']+net['n_bus']:2*net['n_gen']+2*net['n_bus']]\n\n    # Get solver stats\n    stats = solver.stats()\n\n    return {\n        'pg': pg_opt,\n        'qg': qg_opt,\n        'vm': vm_opt,\n        'va': va_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'stats': stats,\n        'success': stats['success'],\n    }\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(net, sol):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from solution\"\"\"\n\n    vm = sol['vm']\n    va = sol['va']\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n        t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n        # Branch admittance\n        y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n        g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n        b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n        g_shunt_f = 0.0\n        b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n        g_shunt_t = 0.0\n        b_shunt_t = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n        tap_mag =    data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef parse_network_data(data):\n    \"\"\"Parse network data into structured format\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    bus =     c2[i] = gencost[i, 4] * baseMVA**2  # $/MW^2/h -> $/pu^2/h\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] * baseMVA      # $/MW/h -> $/pu/h\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6]                # $/h\n            elif ncost[i] == 2:  # Linear\n                c2[i] = 0\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 4] * baseMVA\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 5]\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branch =    # Pg, Qg for each generator (real power, reactive power)\n    pg = ca.MX.sym('pg', net['n_gen'])\n    qg = ca.MX.sym('qg', net['n_gen'])\n\n    # Vm, Va for each bus (voltage magnitude, angle)\n    vm = ca.MX.sym('vm', net['n_bus'])\n    va = ca.MX.sym('va', net['n_bus'])\n\n    # Collect all variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(pg, qg, vm, va)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    # sum_k c2_k * pg_k^2 + c1_k * pg_k + c0_k\n    obj = 0\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            obj += net['c2'][k] * pg[k]**2 + net['c1'][k] * pg[k] + constraint: va[ref] = 0\n    g.append(va[net['ref_bus']])\n    lbg.append(0)\n    ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Create bus-to-generator mapping\n    bus_gens = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n    for k in bus_branches_to[t_idx].append(b)\n\n    # Power balance equations at each bus\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        # Sum of generation at bus i\n        p_gen = 0\n        q_gen = 0\n        for k in bus_gens[i]:\n            if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n                p_gen += pg[k]\n                q_gen += qg[k]\n\n        # Load at bus i\n        p_load = net['pd'][i]\n        q_load = net['qd'][i]\n\n        # Shunt at bus i: S_shunt = (G - jB) * |V|^2\n        # So P_shunt = G * |V|^2, Q_shunt = -B * |V|^2\n        p_shunt = net['gs'][i] * vm[i]**2\n        q_shunt = -net['bs'][i] * vm[i]**2\n\n        # Sum of branch flows from bus i\n        p_flow = 0\n        q_flow = 0\n\n        # Branches where i is the \"from\" bus\n        for b in bus_branches_from[i]:\n            f_idx = i\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            # Branch admittance: y = 1/(r + jx) = (r - jx)/(r^2 + x^2)\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n            # Shunt admittance at from side\n            g_shunt_f = 0.0\n            b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            # Complex transformation ratio: T = tap * exp(j*shift)\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n            tap_mag_sq =      va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ij = ((g_series + g_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) - b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            p_flow += p_ij\n            q_flow += q_ij\n\n        # Branches where i is the \"to\" bus\n        for b in bus_branches_to[i]:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = i\n\n            # Branch admittance\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n            # Shunt admittance at to side\n            g_shunt_t = 0.0\n            b_shunt_t = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n\n            # S_ji = (Y_ij + Y^c_ji)^* * |V_j|^2 - Y_ij^* * V_j * V_i^* / T_ij^*\n            # P_ji = (g+g_sh) * vm_j^2 - vm_i*vm_j/tap * (g*cos(va_i-va_j-shift) - b*sin(va_i-va_j-shift))\n            # Q_ji = -(b+b_sh) * vm_j^2 - vm_i*vm_j/tap * (g*sin(va_i-va_j-shift) + b*cos(va_i-va_j-shift))\n\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ji = ((g_series + g_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) - b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) + b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            p_flow += p_ji\n            q_flow += q_ji\n\n        # Power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n        g.append(p_gen - p_load - p_shunt - p_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(q_gen - q_load - q_shunt - q_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n    x0 = []\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            lbx.append(net['pmin'][k])\n            ubx.append(net['pmax'][k])\n            x0.append(net['pg_init'][k])\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            lbx.append(net['qmin'][k])\n            ubx.append(net['qmax'][k])\n            x0.append(net['qg_init'][k])\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        lbx.append(net['vmin'][i])\n        ubx.append(net['vmax'][i])\n        # Use flat start at 1.0 pu if initial value is invalid\n        vm_start = net['vm_init'][i] if 0.5 < net['vm_init'][i] < 1.5 else 1.0\n        x0.append(vm_start)\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (use wide bounds, except ref bus)\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n        # Flat start at 0 degrees for all buses\n        x0.append(0.0)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1 and net['rate_a'][b] > 0:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            # Compute branch flow magnitude\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            g_shunt_f = 0.0\n            b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) + b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ij = (-(b_series + b_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) - b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            s_ij_mag = ca.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n\n            h.append(s_ij_mag)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(net['rate_a'][b])\n\n            # Also add reverse direction\n            g_shunt_t = 0.0\n            b_shunt_t = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            p_ji = ((g_series + g_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) - b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) + b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            s_ji_mag = ca.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n            h.append(s_ji_mag)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(net['rate_a'][b])\n\n    # Angle difference limits\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1:\n            f_idx =          lbh.append(net['angmin'][b])\n            ubh.append(net['angmax'][b])\n\n    # Concatenate constraints\n    g_all = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h_all = ca.vertcat(*h) if h else    'lbg': np.array(lbg_all),\n        'ubg': np.array(ubg_all),\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(net):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Building AC OPF problem...\")\n    nlp = build_acopf(net)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {nlp['x'].shape[0]} variables, {nlp['g'].shape[0]} constraints\")\n\n    # Create NLP solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 15,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.linear_solver': 'mumps',\n        'ipopt.bound_push': 1e-2,\n        'ipopt.bound_frac': 1e-2,\n        'ipopt.nlp_scaling_method': 'gradient-based',\n        'print_time': 1,\n    }\n\n    nlp_dict = {\n        'x': nlp['x'],\n        'f': nlp['f'],\n        'g': nlp['g'],\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp_dict, opts)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC OPF...\")\n    sol = solver(\n        x0=nlp['x0'],\n        lbx=nlp['lbx'],\n        ubx=nlp['ubx'],\n        lbg=nlp['lbg'],\n        ubg=nlp['ubg'],\n    )\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n\n    pg_opt = x_opt[0:net['n_gen']]\n    qg_opt = x_opt[net['n_gen']:2*net['n_gen']]\n    vm_opt = x_opt[2*net['n_gen']:2*net['n_gen']+net['n_bus']]\n    va_opt = x_opt[2*net['n_gen']+net['n_bus']:2*net['n_gen']+2*net['n_bus']]\n\n    # Get solver stats\n    stats = solver.stats()\n\n    return {\n        'pg': pg_opt,\n        'qg': qg_opt,\n        'vm': vm_opt,\n        'va': va_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'stats': stats,\n        'success': stats['success'],\n    }\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(net, sol):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from solution\"\"\"\n\n    vm = sol['vm']\n    va = sol['va']\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n        t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n        # Branch admittance\n        y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n        g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n        b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n        g_shunt_f = 0.0\n        b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] /   gen = np.array(data['gen'])\n    n_gen = len(gen)\n    gen_bus = gen[:, 0].astype(int)  # Generator bus\n    pg_init = gen[:, 1] / baseMVA  # Initial P output pu\n    qg_init = gen[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Initial Q output pu\n    qmax = gen[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Max Q pu\n    qmin = gen[:, 4] / baseMVA  # Min Q pu\n    vg = gen[:, 5]  # Voltage setpoint pu\n    gen_status = gen[:, 7].astype(int)  #     c2[i] = gencost[i, 4] * baseMVA**2  # $/MW^2/h -> $/pu^2/h\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 5] * baseMVA      # $/MW/h -> $/pu/h\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 6]                # $/h\n            elif ncost[i] == 2:  # Linear\n                c2[i] = 0\n                c1[i] = gencost[i, 4] * baseMVA\n                c0[i] = gencost[i, 5]\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branch = br_x = branch[:, 3]  # Reactance pu\n    br_b = branch[:, 4]  # Charging susceptance pu\n    rate_a = branch[:, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating -> pu\n    tap = branch[:, 8]  # Tap ratio\n    shift =    # Pg, Qg for each generator (real power, reactive power)\n    pg = ca.MX.sym('pg', net['n_gen'])\n    qg = ca.MX.sym('qg', net['n_gen'])\n\n    # Vm, Va for each bus (voltage magnitude, angle)\n    vm = ca.MX.sym('vm', net['n_bus'])\n    va = ca.MX.sym('va', net['n_bus'])\n\n    # Collect all variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(pg, qg, vm, va)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    # sum_k c2_k * pg_k^2 + c1_k * pg_k + c0_k\n    obj = 0\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            obj += net['c2'][k] * pg[k]**2 + net['c1'][k] * pg[k] + constraint: va[ref] = 0\n    g.append(va[net['ref_bus']])\n    lbg.append(0)\n    ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Create bus-to-generator mapping\n    bus_gens = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n    for k in q_load = net['qd'][i]\n\n        # Shunt at bus i: S_shunt = (G - jB) * |V|^2\n        # So P_shunt = G * |V|^2, Q_shunt = -B * |V|^2\n        p_shunt = net['gs'][i] * vm[i]**2\n        q_shunt = g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n            # Shunt admittance at to side\n            g_shunt_t = 0.0\n            b_shunt_t = ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) + b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            p_flow += p_ji\n            q_flow += q_ji\n\n        # Power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n        g.append(p_gen - p_load - p_shunt - p_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n     va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ij = ((g_series + g_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series *      lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(net['rate_a'][b])\n\n    # Angle difference limits\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1:\n            f_idx =          lbh.append(net['angmin'][b])\n            ubh.append(net['angmax'][b])\n\n    # Concatenate constraints\n    g_all = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h_all = ca.vertcat(*h) if h else ref_bus,\n    }\n\ndef build_acopf(net):\n    \"\"\"Build AC OPF optimization problem using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    # Create optimization variables\n    # Pg, Qg for each generator (real power, reactive power)\n    pg = ca.MX.sym('pg', net['n_gen'])\n    qg = ca.MX.sym('qg', net['n_gen'])\n\n    # Vm, Va for each bus (voltage magnitude, angle)\n    vm = ca.MX.sym('vm', net['n_bus'])\n    va = ca.MX.sym('va', net['n_bus'])\n\n    # Collect all variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(pg, qg, vm, va)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    # sum_k c2_k * pg_k^2 + c1_k * pg_k + c0_k\n    obj = 0\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            obj += net['c2'][k] * pg[k]**2 + net['c1'][k] * pg[k] + net['c0'][k]\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Reference bus angle constraint: va[ref] = 0\n    g.append(va[net['ref_bus']])\n    lbg.append(0)\n    ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Create bus-to-generator mapping\n    bus_gens = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        bus_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['gen_bus'][k])[0][0]\n        bus_gens[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Create bus-to-branch mapping (branches connected to each bus)\n    bus_branches_from = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n    bus_branches_to = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1:\n            f_idx = bus_branches_to[t_idx].append(b)\n\n    # Power balance equations at each bus\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        # Sum of generation at bus i\n        p_gen = 0\n        q_gen = 0\n        for k in bus_gens[i]:\n            if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n                p_gen += pg[k]\n                q_gen += qg[k]\n\n        # Load at bus i\n        p_load = net['pd'][i]\n        q_load = net['qd'][i]\n\n        # Shunt at bus i: S_shunt = (G - jB) * |V|^2\n        # So P_shunt = G * |V|^2, Q_shunt = -B * |V|^2\n        p_shunt = net['gs'][i] * vm[i]**2\n        q_shunt = -net['bs'][i] * vm[i]**2\n\n        # Sum of branch flows from bus i\n        p_flow = 0\n        q_flow = 0\n\n        # Branches where i is the \"from\" bus\n        for b in bus_branches_from[i]:\n            f_idx = i\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            # Branch admittance: y = 1/(r + jx) = (r - jx)/(r^2 + x^2)\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n            # Shunt admittance at from side\n            g_shunt_f = 0.0\n            b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            # Complex transformation ratio: T = tap * exp(j*shift)\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n            tap_mag_sq =      va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ij = ((g_series + g_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) + b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ij = (-(b_series + b_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) - b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            p_flow += p_ij\n            q_flow += q_ij\n\n        # Branches where i is the \"to\" bus\n        for b in bus_branches_to[i]:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = i\n\n            # Branch admittance\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n            # Shunt admittance at to side\n            g_shunt_t = 0.0\n            b_shunt_t = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n\n            # S_ji = (Y_ij + Y^c_ji)^* * |V_j|^2 - Y_ij^* * V_j * V_i^* / T_ij^*\n            # P_ji = (g+g_sh) * vm_j^2 - vm_i*vm_j/tap * (g*cos(va_i-va_j-shift) - b*sin(va_i-va_j-shift))\n            # Q_ji = -(b+b_sh) * vm_j^2 - vm_i*vm_j/tap * (g*sin(va_i-va_j-shift) + b*cos(va_i-va_j-shift))\n\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ji = ((g_series + g_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) - b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) + b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            p_flow += p_ji\n            q_flow += q_ji\n\n        # Power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n        g.append(p_gen - p_load - p_shunt - p_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(q_gen - q_load - q_shunt - q_flow)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n    x0 = []\n\n    # Compute total load for initialization\n    total_load_p = sum(net['pd'])\n    total_pmax = sum(net['pmax'][k] for k in range(net['n_gen']) if net['gen_status'][k] == 1)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            lbx.append(net['pmin'][k])\n            ubx.append(net['pmax'][k])\n            # Initialize with proportional load sharing plus some margin for losses\n            pg_start = net['pmax'][k] * (total_load_p * 1.05) / total_pmax if total_pmax > 0 else net['pmin'][k]\n            pg_start = np.clip(pg_start, net['pmin'][k], net['pmax'][k])\n            x0.append(pg_start)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        if net['gen_status'][k] == 1:\n            lbx.append(net['qmin'][k])\n            ubx.append(net['qmax'][k])\n            # Initialize Q at zero or midpoint\n            qg_start = 0.0\n            qg_start = np.clip(qg_start, net['qmin'][k], net['qmax'][k])\n            x0.append(qg_start)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        lbx.append(net['vmin'][i])\n        ubx.append(net['vmax'][i])\n        # Use flat start at 1.0 pu if initial value is invalid\n        vm_start = net['vm_init'][i] if 0.5 < net['vm_init'][i] < 1.5 else 1.0\n        x0.append(vm_start)\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (use wide bounds, except ref bus)\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        lbx.append(-np.pi)\n        ubx.append(np.pi)\n        # Flat start at 0 degrees for all buses\n        x0.append(0.0)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1 and net['rate_a'][b] > 0:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            # Compute branch flow magnitude\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            g_shunt_f = 0.0\n            b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n            tap_mag_sq = tap_mag**2\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - net['shift'][b]\n\n            p_ij = ((g_series + g_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) + b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ij = (-(b_series + b_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) - b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            # Use squared formulation to avoid sqrt issues\n            s_ij_mag_sq = p_ij**2 + q_ij**2\n\n            h.append(s_ij_mag_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(net['rate_a'][b]**2)\n\n            # Also add reverse direction\n            g_shunt_t = 0.0\n            b_shunt_t = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n            p_ji = ((g_series + g_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.cos(va_diff) - b_series * ca.sin(va_diff)))\n\n            q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                    - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * ca.sin(va_diff) + b_series * ca.cos(va_diff)))\n\n            s_ji_mag_sq = p_ji**2 + q_ji**2\n\n            h.append(s_ji_mag)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(net['rate_a'][b])\n\n    # Angle difference limits\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 1:\n            f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx]\n\n            h.append(va_diff)\n            lbh.append(net['angmin'][b])\n            ubh.append(net['angmax'][b])\n\n    # Concatenate constraints\n    g_all = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h_all = ca.vertcat(*h) if h else ca.MX()\n\n    # Combine all constraints\n    if g and h:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(g_all, h_all)\n        lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n        ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n    elif g:\n        constraints = g_all\n        lbg_all = lbg\n        ubg_all = ubg\n    elif h:\n        constraints = h_all\n        lbg_all = lbh\n        ubg_all = ubh\n    else:\n        constraints = ca.MX()\n        lbg_all = []\n        ubg_all = []\n\n    return {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': constraints,\n        'x0': np.array(x0),\n        'lbx': np.array(lbx),\n        'ubx': np.array(ubx),\n        'lbg': np.array(lbg_all),\n        'ubg': np.array(ubg_all),\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(net):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Building AC OPF problem...\")\n    nlp = build_acopf(net)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {nlp['x'].shape[0]} variables, {nlp['g'].shape[0]} constraints\")\n\n    # Create NLP solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 15,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.linear_solver': 'mumps',\n        'ipopt.bound_push': 1e-2,\n        'ipopt.bound_frac': 1e-2,\n        'ipopt.nlp_scaling_method': 'gradient-based',\n        'print_time': 1,\n    }\n\n    nlp_dict = {\n        'x': nlp['x'],\n        'f': nlp['f'],\n        'g': nlp['g'],\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp_dict, opts)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC OPF...\")\n    sol = solver(\n        x0=nlp['x0'],\n        lbx=nlp['lbx'],\n        ubx=nlp['ubx'],\n        lbg=nlp['lbg'],\n        ubg=nlp['ubg'],\n    )\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n\n    pg_opt = x_opt[0:net['n_gen']]\n    qg_opt = x_opt[net['n_gen']:2*net['n_gen']]\n    vm_opt = x_opt[2*net['n_gen']:2*net['n_gen']+net['n_bus']]\n    va_opt = x_opt[2*net['n_gen']+net['n_bus']:2*net['n_gen']+2*net['n_bus']]\n\n    # Get solver stats\n    stats = solver.stats()\n\n    return {\n        'pg': pg_opt,\n        'qg': qg_opt,\n        'vm': vm_opt,\n        'va': va_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'stats': stats,\n        'success': stats['success'],\n    }\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(net, sol):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from solution\"\"\"\n\n    vm = sol['vm']\n    va = sol['va']\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for b in range(net['n_branch']):\n        if net['br_status'][b] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['f_bus'][b])[0][0]\n        t_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n        # Branch admittance\n        y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n        g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n        b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n\n        g_shunt_f = 0.0\n        b_shunt_f = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n        g_shunt_t = 0.0\n        b_shunt_t = net['br_b'][b] / 2\n\n        tap_mag = net['tap'][b]\n        tap_mag_sq = tap_mag**2\n        shift = net['shift'][b]\n\n        va_diff = va[f_idx] - va[t_idx] - shift\n\n        # From side flow\n        p_ij = ((g_series + g_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * np.cos(va_diff) + b_series * np.sin(va_diff)))\n\n        q_ij = (-(b_series + b_shunt_f) * vm[f_idx]**2 / tap_mag_sq\n                - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * np.sin(va_diff) - b_series * np.cos(va_diff)))\n\n        s_ij = np.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n\n        # To side flow\n        p_ji = ((g_series + g_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * np.cos(va_diff) - b_series * np.sin(va_diff)))\n\n        q_ji = (-(b_series + b_shunt_t) * vm[t_idx]**2\n                - vm[f_idx] * vm[t_idx] / tap_mag * (g_series * np.sin(va_diff) + b_series * np.cos(va_diff)))\n\n        s_ji = np.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n        # Loading percentage\n        if net['rate_a'][b] > 0:\n            loading_pct = max(s_ij, s_ji) / net['rate_a'][b] * 100\n        else:\n            loading_pct = 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'branch_idx': b,\n            'from_bus': int(net['f_bus'][b]),\n            'to_bus': int(net['t_bus'][b]),\n            'p_from': p_ij * net['baseMVA'],\n            'q_from': q_ij * net['baseMVA'],\n            's_from': s_ij * net['baseMVA'],\n            'p_to': p_ji * net['baseMVA'],\n            'q_to': q_ji * net['baseMVA'],\n            's_to': s_ji * net['baseMVA'],\n            'limit': net['rate_a'][b] * net['baseMVA'],\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef check_feasibility(net, sol):\n    \"\"\"Check solution feasibility\"\"\"\n\n    vm = sol['vm']\n    va = sol['va']\n    pg = sol['pg']\n    qg = sol['qg']\n\n    # Create bus-to-generator mapping\n    bus_gens = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n    for k in range(net['n_gen']):\n        bus_idx = np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['gen_bus'][k])[0][0]\n        bus_gens[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Create bus-to-branch mapping\n    bus_branches_from = [[] for _ in range(net['n_bus'])]\n    bus_branches_to = [[] for _ in np.where(net['bus_i'] == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n            bus_branches_from[f_idx].append(b)\n            bus_branches_to[t_idx].append(b)\n\n    # Check power balance\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i in range(net['n_bus']):\n        # Generation\n        p_gen = sum(pg[k] for k in bus_gens[i] if net['gen_status'][k] == 1)\n        q_gen = sum(qg[k] for k in bus_gens[i] if net['gen_status'][k] == 1)\n\n        # Load\n        p_load = net['pd'][i]\n        q_load = net['qd'][i]\n\n        # Shunt\n        p_shunt = net['gs'][i] * vm[i]**2\n        q_shunt == net['t_bus'][b])[0][0]\n\n            y_mag_sq = 1.0 / (net['br_r'][b]**2 + net['br_x'][b]**2)\n            g_series = net['br_r'][b] * y_mag_sq\n            b_series = -net['br_x'][b] * y_mag_sq\n Create Pandapower network\n    net = pp.create_empty_network()\n    net.sn_mva = data['baseMVA']\n\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gen_data = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branch_data = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencost_data = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    # Create generators\n    for i, gen in enumerate(gen_data):\n        gen_bus = int(gen[0])\n        pg_mw = gen[1]\n        qg_mvar = gen[2]\n        qmax_mvar = gen[3]\n        qmin_mvar = gen[4]\n        vg_pu = gen[5]\n        gen_status = int(gen[7])\n        pmax_mw = gen[8]\n        pmin_mw = gen[9]\n\n        if gen_status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        pp_bus_idx = bus_mapping[gen_bus]\n        bus_type = int(bus_data[np.where(bus_data[:, 0] == gen_bus)[0][0], 1])\n\n        # Extract cost coefficients\n        cost_model = int(gencost_data[i, 0])\n        if cost_model == 2:  # Polynomial\n            ncost = int(gencost_data[i, 3])\n            if ncost == 3:\n                c2 = gencost_data[i, 4]\n                c1 = gencost_data[i, 5]\n                c0 = gencost_data[i, 6]\n            elif ncost == 2:\n                c2 = 0\n                c1 = gencost_data[i, 4]\n                c0 = gencost_data[i, 5]\n            else:\n                c2 = 0\n                c1 = 0\n                c0 = gencost_data[i, 6] if ncost > 0 else 0\n        else:\n            c2 = 0\n            c1 = 0\n            c0 = 0\n\n        # Add polynomial cost\n        pp.create_poly_cost(net, i, 'gen', cp2_eur_per_mw2=c2, cp1_eur_per_mw=c1, cp0_eur=c0)\n\n    # Create branches (lines and transformers)\n    for i, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        f_bus = int(branch[0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[1])\n        r_pu = branch[2]\n        x_pu = branch[3]\n        b_pu = branch[4]\n        rate_a_mva = branch[5]\n        tap = branch[8]\n        shift_deg = branch[9]\n        br_status = int(branch[10])\n        angmin_deg = branch[11]\n        angmax_deg = branch[12]\n\n        if br_status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        pp_f_bus = bus_mapping[f_bus]\n        pp_t_bus = bus_mapping[t_bus]\n\n        # Get base voltage\n        from_vn_kv = net.bus.at[pp_f_bus, 'vn_kv']\n\n        # Convert pu impedance to ohms\n        z_base_ohm = (from_vn_kv ** 2) / net.sn_mva\n        r_ohm_per_km = r_pu * z_base_ohm\n        x_ohm_per_km = x_pu * z_base_ohm\n        c_nf_per_km = b_pu * 1e9 / (2 * np.pi * 50 * z_base_ohm) if abs(b_pu) > 1e-10 else 0\n\n        # Check if this is a transformer (tap != 0 and != 1)\n        is_trafo = (abs(tap) > 1e-6 and abs(tap - 1.0) > 1e-6) or abs(shift_deg) > 1e-6\n\n        if is_trafo:\n            # Create transformer\n            if tap == 0:\n                tap = 1.0\n\n            to_vn_kv = net.bus.at[pp_t_bus, 'vn_kv']\n\n            # For pandapower, we need to create a standard transformer\n            # The tap ratio is on the HV side by default\n            pp.create_transformer_from_parameters(\n                net,\n                hv_bus=pp_f_bus,\n                lv_bus=pp_t_bus,\n                sn_mva=net.sn_mva if rate_a_mva == 0 else rate_a_mva,\n                vn_hv_kv=from_vn_kv,\n                vn_lv_kv=to_vn_kv,\n                vkr_percent=r_pu * 100,\n                vk_percent=np.sqrt(r_pu**2 + x_pu**2) * 100,\n                pfe_kw=0,\n                i0_percent=0,\n                shift_degree=shift_deg,\n                tap_pos=0,\n                tap_neutral=0,\n                tap_min=-10,\n                tap_max=10,\n                tap_step_percent=100 * (tap - 1.0) / 10 if abs(tap - 1.0) > 1e-6 else 0,\n                tap_side=\"hv\",\n                max_loading_percent=100 if rate_a_mva == 0 else None,\n                in_service=True,\n                name=f\"Trafo {i+1}\",\n            )\n        else:\n            # Create line\n            length_km = 1.0  # Use 1 km as reference\n\n            max_i_ka = rate_a_mva * 1000 / (np.sqrt(3) * from_vn_kv) if rate_a_mva > 0 else None\n\n            pp.create_line_from_parameters(\n                net,\n                from_bus=pp_f_bus,\n                to_bus=pp_t_bus,\n                length_km=length_km,\n                r_ohm_per_km=r_ohm_per_km,\n                x_ohm_per_km=x_ohm_per_km,\n                c_nf_per_km=c_nf_per_km,\n                max_i_ka=max_i_ka,\n                max_loading_percent=100 if rate_a_mva == 0 else None,\n                in_service=True,\n                name=f\"Line {i+1}\",\n            )\n\n    return net\n\ndef solve_opf(net):\n    \"\"\"Solve OPF using Pandapower\"\"\"\n\n    print(f\"Network: {len(net.bus)} buses, {len(net.gen)} generators, {len(net.line)} lines, {len(net.trafo)} transformers\")\n\n    try:\n        # Run OPF\n        print(\"\\nSolving OPF...\")\n        pp.runopp(net, delta=1e-10, verbose=True)\n\n        print(\"\\nOPF converged successfully!\")\n        print(f\"Total cost: {net.res_cost:.2f} EUR/h\")\n\n        return net, True\n\n    except OPFNotConverged as e:\n        print(f\"\\nOPF did not converge: {e}\")\n        # Try running a simple power flow first\n        print(\"Trying simple power flow...\")\n        try:\n            pp.runpp(net, algorithm='nr', calculate_voltage_angles=True)\n            print(\"Power flow converged. Network is AC feasible.\")\n            return net, False\n        except:\n            print(\"Power flow also failed. Network may be infeasible.\")\n            return net, False\n\ndef generate_report(net, opf_success):\n    \"\"\"Generate report from Pandapower results\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = net.sn_mva\n\n    # Summary\n    if opf_success:\n        total_cost = net.res_cost\n        solver_status = \"optimal\"\n    else:\n        total_cost = 0\n        solver_status = \"failed\"\n\n    total_load_mw = net.res_sgen['p_mw'].sum()  # sgen is negative load\n        total_load_mvar -= net.res_sgen['q_mvar'].sum()\n\n    total_loss_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    summary = {\n        'total_cost_per_hour': total_cost,\n        'total_load_MW': total_load_mw,\n        'total_load_MVAr': total_load_mvar,\n        'total_generation_MW': total_gen_mw,\n        'total_generation_MVAr': 'vmin_pu': bus_row['min_vm_pu'],\n            'vmax_pu': bus_row['max_vm_pu'],\n        })\n\n    # Branch flows (lines and transformers)\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    # Lines\n    for idx in 'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n            'flow_from_MVA': s_from,\n            'flow_to_MVA': s_to,\n            'limit_MVA': limit_mva,\n        })\n\n    # Transformers\n    for idx in 'limit_MVA': limit_mva,\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading and get top 10\n    branch_flows.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_flows[:10]\n\n    # Convert to 1-indexed\n            'pg_MW': res_row['p_mw'],\n            'qg_MVAr': res_row['q_mvar'],\n            'pmin_MW': gen_row['min_p_mw'],\n            'pmax_MW': gen_row['max_p_mw'],\n            'qmin_MVAr': gen_row['min_q_mvar'],\n            'qmax_MVAr': gen_row['max_q_mvar'],\n        })\n\n    # Get apparent power\n        s_from = np.sqrt(res_row['p_from_mw']**2 + res_row['q_from_mvar']**2)\n        s_to = np.sqrt(res_row['p_to_mw']**2 + res_row['q_to_mvar']**2)\n\n        # Get limit\n        if pd.notna(line_row['max_i_ka']) and line_row['max_i_ka'] > 0:\n            vn_kv = net.bus.at[line_row['from_bus'], 'vn_kv']\n            limit_mva = np.sqrt(3) * line_row['max_i_ka'] * vn_kv\n        else:\n            limit_mva = baseMVA * 10  # Default large value\n\n        loading_pct = res_row['loading_percent'] if pd.notna(res_row['loading_percent']) else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': from_bus,\n            'to_bus': to_bus,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n            'flow_from_MVA': s_from,\n            'flow_to_MVA': s_to,\n            'limit_MVA': limit_mva,\n        })\n\n    # Check voltage violations\n    for idx in net.bus.index:\n        bus_row = net.bus.loc[idx]\n        res_row = net.res_bus.loc[idx]\n        vm = res_row['vm_pu']\n        vmin = bus_row['min_vm_pu']\n        vmax = bus_row['max_vm_pu']\n\n        if vm < vmin:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, vmin - vm)\n        if vm > vmax:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, vm - vmax)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows using power flow equations\n        vm_f = bus[f_idx, VM]\n        va_f = bus[f_idx, VA] * np.pi / 180\n        vm_t = bus[t_idx, VM]\n        va_t = bus[t_idx, VA] * np.pi / 180\n\n        r = branch[i, BR_R]\n        x = branch[i, BR_X]\n        b = branch[i, BR_B]\n        tap = branch[i, TAP] if branch[i, TAP] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[i, SHIFT] * np.pi / 180\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        b_sh = b / 2\n\n        # Voltage angle difference\n        theta = va_f - va_t - shift\n\n        # From-side flow\n        p_from = (g + 0) / tap**2 * vm_f**2 - vm_f * vm_t / tap * (g * np.cos(theta) + b_series * np.sin(theta))\n        q_from = -(b_series + b_sh) / tap**2 * vm_f**2 - vm_f * vm_t / tap * (g * np.sin(theta) - b_series * np.cos(theta))\n\n        # Loading percentage\n        if limit_mva > 0:\n            loading_pct = max(s_from, s_to) / limit_mva * 100\n        else:\n            loading_pct = 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': f_bus_num,\n            'to_bus': t_bus_num,\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(s_from),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(s_to),\n            'limit_MVA': float(limit_mva),\n        })\n\n    # PYPOWER ensures power balance\n        'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.0,\n        'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_v_violation),\n        'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload),\n    }\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': summary,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': feasibility_check,\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network from JSON...\")\n    ppc = load_network_from_json('network.json')\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving OPF...\")\n    results, success = solve_opf(ppc)\n\n    print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n    report = generate_report(results, success)\n\n    with open('report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\nReport saved to report.json\")\n\n    if success:\n        print(f\"\\nSummary:\")\n        print(f\"  Status: {report['summary']['solver_status']}\")\n        print(f\"  Total cost: ${report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:.2f}/hour\")\n        print(f\"  Total generation: {report['summary']['total_generation_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"  Total load: {report['summary']['total_load_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"  Total losses: {report['summary']['total_losses_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"\\nFeasibility:\")\n        print(f\"  Max voltage violation: {report['feasibility_check']['max_voltage_violation_pu']:.6f} pu\")\n        print(f\"  Max branch overload: {report['feasibility_check']['max_branch_overload_MVA']:.2f} MVA\")\n\n    return 0 if success else 1\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    sys.exit(main())\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":144,"oldLines":12,"newStart":144,"newLines":9,"lines":["             'vmax_pu': float(bus[i, VMAX]),","         })"," ","-    #
